HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1fc697be68af37b1b324","text":"3. **Enclosure like property pen or dwelling threshold.** Lexical evidence: و ص د B002 “house forecourt/doorway joined to dwelling,” B003 “stone pen for livestock/property,” B004 “plants with roots close together.” Structural cue: the surah moved through city, wealth/property, necks in bondage, and companions. Assigned role: مؤصدة makes a closed compound around the group. Abductive move: I infer that the same social world of possession and enclosure culminates in being enclosed. Reading change: “upon them” becomes spatially heavy: a locked structure comes down over those who did not loosen others.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B001","root_001653/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_53da7017657916100469","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"90:20:3\",\"root_001653\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Mühürlü yer, çıkışın, misafirperverliğin ve koruyucu eşliğin bulunmadığı yaşanamaz bir kapalı mekândır; açık ilişkiyi reddetmek böyle bir barınmayla sonuçlanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6c2c723f105cc128b597","text":"1. **Fire as sealed counter-opening.** Lexical evidence: ن و ر B002 “burning fire,” و ص د B001 “door sealed shut”; against ف ك ك B001/B002 in 90:13 “opening/freeing.” Structural cue: the surah’s first pass-act opens a bound neck, while the final image closes over the rejecters. Assigned role: fire burns; wasd seals the exit. Abductive move: I infer moral reversal: those who refused opening practices are placed under closure. Reading change: the final fire is not only heat; it is locked consequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B002","root_001564/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_860966d36fd7e4284607","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"90:20:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B002\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yol gösteren ışık olabilecek parlaklık, çıkışı göstermeyen yakıcı bir işarete dönüşerek rehberliği tersine çevirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000074/B003","root_001564/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a76827cc26f854a49d4d","text":"2. **Lost illumination becomes burning enclosure.** Lexical evidence: ن و ر B001 “light/illumination,” B005 “beacon/landmark,” B002 “fire”; ء ي ي B003 visible sign in 90:19. Structural cue: signs, eyes, and guidance all precede this fire. Assigned role: نور’s light/marker potential is inverted into fire. Abductive move: I infer that unaccepted guidance/sign-light returns as destructive brightness. Reading change: the final نار is an anti-guidance: illumination without exit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bf640e20193dd18ba875","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"90:20:3\",\"root_001653\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Mühürlü ateş, malı ve hayvanı içeride tutan kapalı bir ağıl gibi, başkalarını sahiplik altında tutanları da kuşatan bir kapana dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e2a30671f01c4f3a6ebe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"90:20:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ateş, merhametli ilişkilere karşı sürdürülen toplumsal düşmanlığın maddi bir çevreye dönüşmüş biçimidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001","root_001564/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f6285e53e95f496f1a6d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"90:20:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateş, önceki örtmenin karşılığı olarak gizlenen sonucu zorla görünür kılan bir aydınlanmaya da dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f93376c5cef4b29bf68e","text":"The ending illuminated the whole ayah-walk by reversal: basmalah mercy versus sealed fire, حِلّ/open status versus مؤصدة, فَكّ/opening versus closure, guidance-light versus burning enclosure.
